03.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/install-as-app must return only fields permitted by the
caller’s administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose
pagination metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request
against the latest resource version and must return an operation
identifier when work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s
standard error envelope and must not expose provider credentials or
protected implementation details.

03.AUDIT EVENTS

03.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 04: DATABASE
================================================================================

The Database page is an administrative surface for schemas, tables,
indexes, migrations, records, backups, query tools, retention, access
policies, and database health. It must operate within the selected
tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never become a
normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/database. The server
must require the dedicated permission for Database before returning page
data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint
used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

04.001 Database requirement 1: Require confirmation text or an explicit
secondary confirmation for irreversible operations when appropriate. For
this page, apply that rule specifically to schemas, tables, indexes,
migrations, records, backups, query tools, retention, access policies,
and database health, using the selected database context where
applicable. 04.002 Database requirement 2: Offer a safe preview or diff
for configuration changes that alter runtime behavior. For this page,
apply that rule specifically to schemas, tables, indexes, migrations,
records, backups, query tools, retention, access policies, and database
health, using the selected database context where applicable. 04.003
Database requirement 3: Validate referenced resources before saving
relationships so broken dependencies cannot be silently stored. For this
page, apply that rule specifically to schemas, tables, indexes,
migrations, records, backups, query tools, retention, access policies,
and database health, using the selected database context where
applicable. 04.004 Database requirement 4: Use transactional writes when
multiple records must change together; otherwise expose partial-failure
behavior honestly. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
schemas, tables, indexes, migrations, records, backups, query tools,
retention, access policies, and database health, using the selected
database context where applicable. 04.005 Database requirement 5:
Provide retry controls only for operations known to be safe to retry or
protected by idempotency. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
schemas, tables, indexes, migrations, records, backups, query tools,
retention, access policies, and database health, using the selected
database context where applicable. 04.006 Database requirement 6: Expose
health diagnostics separately from remediation actions so observing a

specifically to schemas, tables, indexes, migrations, records, backups,

selected database context where applicable. 04.007 Database requirement

04.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/database must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

04.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 05: FILES
================================================================================

The Files page is an administrative surface for global file inventory,
quarantine, ownership, permissions, previews, version history,
retention, scanning, and administrative file actions. It must operate
within the selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and
must never become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/files. The server must
require the dedicated permission for Files before returning page data.
The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint used by
the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

05.001 Files requirement 1: Use transactional writes when multiple
records must change together; otherwise expose partial-failure behavior
honestly. For this page, apply that rule specifically to global file
inventory, quarantine, ownership, permissions, previews, version
history, retention, scanning, and administrative file actions, using the
selected files context where applicable. 05.002 Files requirement 2:
Provide retry controls only for operations known to be safe to retry or
protected by idempotency. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
global file inventory, quarantine, ownership, permissions, previews,
version history, retention, scanning, and administrative file actions,
using the selected files context where applicable. 05.003 Files
requirement 3: Expose health diagnostics separately from remediation
actions so observing a fault cannot accidentally mutate it. For this

05.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/files must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

05.SEC SECURITY MODEL

05.UX STATES

05.AUDIT EVENTS

05.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 06: STORAGE
================================================================================

The Storage page is an administrative surface for buckets, volumes,
quotas, lifecycle policies, replication, object metadata, cleanup,
capacity alerts, and provider configuration. It must operate within the
selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never
become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/storage. The server
must require the dedicated permission for Storage before returning page
data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint
used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

06.001 Storage requirement 1: Keep sensitive values masked and provide
rotation or replacement flows rather than routine plaintext viewing. For
this page, apply that rule specifically to buckets, volumes, quotas,
lifecycle policies, replication, object metadata, cleanup, capacity
alerts, and provider configuration, using the selected storage context
where applicable. 06.002 Storage requirement 2: Respect environment
boundaries when cloning configuration and require explicit destination
confirmation. For this page, apply that rule specifically to buckets,
volumes, quotas, lifecycle policies, replication, object metadata,
cleanup, capacity alerts, and provider configuration, using the selected
storage context where applicable. 06.003 Storage requirement 3: Support
import/export through validated schemas with size, type, and

buckets, volumes, quotas, lifecycle policies, replication, object

the selected storage context where applicable. 06.004 Storage

06.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/storage must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

06.SEC SECURITY MODEL

06.UX STATES

06.AUDIT EVENTS

06.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 07: ADD-ONS
================================================================================

The Add-ons page is an administrative surface for feature packages,
extensions, entitlement rules, versions, compatibility, configuration,
installation, upgrades, and rollback. It must operate within the
selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never
become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/add-ons. The server
must require the dedicated permission for Add-ons before returning page
data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint
used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

07.001 Add-ons requirement 1: Prevent unauthorized cross-tenant
references when administrators search globally. For this page, apply
that rule specifically to feature packages, extensions, entitlement
rules, versions, compatibility, configuration, installation, upgrades,
and rollback, using the selected add-ons context where applicable.

07.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/add-ons must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

07.SEC SECURITY MODEL

07.AUDIT EVENTS

07.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 08: BOTS & AGENTS
================================================================================

The Bots & Agents page is an administrative surface for global agents,
bot templates, tools, policies, versions, runtime settings, deployment
state, permissions, and agent governance. It must operate within the
selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never
become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/bots-and-agents. The
server must require the dedicated permission for Bots & Agents before
returning page data. The permission check must be repeated by every
mutation endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for
UX but is never sufficient for authorization.

08.001 Bots & Agents requirement 1: Expose machine-readable error codes
internally while presenting actionable human-readable messages in the
UI. For this page, apply that rule specifically to global agents, bot
templates, tools, policies, versions, runtime settings, deployment
state, permissions, and agent governance, using the selected bots &
agents context where applicable. 08.002 Bots & Agents requirement 2:
Avoid revealing whether a protected resource exists when the viewer
lacks permission to enumerate it. For this page, apply that rule
specifically to global agents, bot templates, tools, policies, versions,
runtime settings, deployment state, permissions, and agent governance,
using the selected bots & agents context where applicable. 08.003 Bots &
Agents requirement 3: Cache non-sensitive metadata with explicit

08.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/bots-and-agents must return only fields permitted by the
caller’s administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose
pagination metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request
against the latest resource version and must return an operation
identifier when work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s
standard error envelope and must not expose provider credentials or
protected implementation details.

08.SEC SECURITY MODEL

08.AUDIT EVENTS

08.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 09: WORKFLOWS
================================================================================

The Workflows page is an administrative surface for automation
definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies, execution
history, retries, approvals, and workflow governance. It must operate
within the selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and
must never become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/workflows. The server
must require the dedicated permission for Workflows before returning
page data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation
endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but
is never sufficient for authorization.

09.001 Workflows requirement 1: Use background workers for expensive
scans, rebuilds, imports, exports, migrations, and analytics
calculations. For this page, apply that rule specifically to automation
definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies, execution
history, retries, approvals, and workflow governance, using the selected
workflows context where applicable. 09.002 Workflows requirement 2: Show
worker queue status and last successful execution where that information
is safe and relevant. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
automation definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies,
execution history, retries, approvals, and workflow governance, using
the selected workflows context where applicable. 09.003 Workflows
requirement 3: Prevent browser refresh or navigation from accidentally
repeating a privileged mutation. For this page, apply that rule
specifically to automation definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes,
dependencies, execution history, retries, approvals, and workflow
governance, using the selected workflows context where applicable.
09.004 Workflows requirement 4: Require current-context verification
immediately before high-impact operations whose target may have changed.
For this page, apply that rule specifically to automation definitions,
triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies, execution history, retries,
approvals, and workflow governance, using the selected workflows context
where applicable. 09.005 Workflows requirement 5: Support notification
hooks for failures without putting secrets or sensitive payloads into
notification content. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
automation definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies,
execution history, retries, approvals, and workflow governance, using
the selected workflows context where applicable. 09.006 Workflows
requirement 6: Make configuration history searchable when versioning is
enabled and show which actor introduced each version. For this page,
apply that rule specifically to automation definitions, triggers,
schedules, nodes, dependencies, execution history, retries, approvals,
and workflow governance, using the selected workflows context where

definitions, triggers, schedules, nodes, dependencies, execution

workflows context where applicable. 09.008 Workflows requirement 8: Keep

editing historical events. For this page, apply that rule specifically

09.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/workflows must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

09.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 10: VOICE
================================================================================

The Voice page is an administrative surface for voice providers, speech
settings, voices, languages, limits, routing, transcription, synthesis,
quality controls, and voice governance. It must operate within the
selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never
become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/voice. The server must
require the dedicated permission for Voice before returning page data.
The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint used by
the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

10.001 Voice requirement 1: Require current-context verification
immediately before high-impact operations whose target may have changed.
For this page, apply that rule specifically to voice providers, speech
settings, voices, languages, limits, routing, transcription, synthesis,
quality controls, and voice governance, using the selected voice context
where applicable. 10.002 Voice requirement 2: Support notification hooks
for failures without putting secrets or sensitive payloads into
notification content. For this page, apply that rule specifically to

10.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/voice must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

10.AUDIT EVENTS

10.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 11: SKILLS
================================================================================

The Skills page is an administrative surface for reusable skills,
schemas, prompts, tools, dependencies, versions, publication status,
permissions, testing, and lifecycle management. It must operate within
the selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must
never become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/skills. The server must
require the dedicated permission for Skills before returning page data.
The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint used by
the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

11.001 Skills requirement 1: Allow rollback only after compatibility
checks against the current environment and dependency graph. For this
page, apply that rule specifically to reusable skills, schemas, prompts,
tools, dependencies, versions, publication status, permissions, testing,
and lifecycle management, using the selected skills context where
applicable. 11.002 Skills requirement 2: Keep audit records
append-oriented and prevent ordinary administrators from editing
historical events. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
reusable skills, schemas, prompts, tools, dependencies, versions,
publication status, permissions, testing, and lifecycle management,
using the selected skills context where applicable. 11.003 Skills
requirement 3: Ensure deletion flows identify dependent resources and
explain whether cascading removal is supported. For this page, apply
that rule specifically to reusable skills, schemas, prompts, tools,
dependencies, versions, publication status, permissions, testing, and
lifecycle management, using the selected skills context where
applicable. 11.004 Skills requirement 4: Use soft deletion where
appropriate for recoverable administrative resources, with explicit
purge controls for authorized roles. For this page, apply that rule
specifically to reusable skills, schemas, prompts, tools, dependencies,
versions, publication status, permissions, testing, and lifecycle
management, using the selected skills context where applicable. 11.005
Skills requirement 5: Enforce rate limits on expensive administrative
endpoints and provide a retry-after signal when throttled. For this
page, apply that rule specifically to reusable skills, schemas, prompts,
tools, dependencies, versions, publication status, permissions, testing,
and lifecycle management, using the selected skills context where
applicable. 11.006 Skills requirement 6: Record provider request
identifiers when returned so operational incidents can be traced without
exposing tokens. For this page, apply that rule specifically to reusable

11.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/skills must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

11.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 12: TEACH THE AI
================================================================================

The Teach the AI page is an administrative surface for global
instructions, knowledge sources, behavior rules, retrieval sources,
evaluation sets, versioning, approvals, and rollback. It must operate
within the selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and
must never become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/teach-the-ai. The
server must require the dedicated permission for Teach the AI before
returning page data. The permission check must be repeated by every
mutation endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for
UX but is never sufficient for authorization.

12.001 Teach the AI requirement 1: Use soft deletion where appropriate
for recoverable administrative resources, with explicit purge controls
for authorized roles. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
global instructions, knowledge sources, behavior rules, retrieval
sources, evaluation sets, versioning, approvals, and rollback, using the
selected teach the ai context where applicable. 12.002 Teach the AI
requirement 2: Enforce rate limits on expensive administrative endpoints
and provide a retry-after signal when throttled. For this page, apply
that rule specifically to global instructions, knowledge sources,
behavior rules, retrieval sources, evaluation sets, versioning,
approvals, and rollback, using the selected teach the ai context where
applicable. 12.003 Teach the AI requirement 3: Record provider request
identifiers when returned so operational incidents can be traced without

evaluation sets, versioning, approvals, and rollback, using the selected
teach the ai context where applicable. 12.004 Teach the AI requirement

applicable. 12.005 Teach the AI requirement 5: Make the page resilient

12.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/teach-the-ai must return only fields permitted by the
caller’s administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose
pagination metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request
against the latest resource version and must return an operation
identifier when work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s
standard error envelope and must not expose provider credentials or
protected implementation details.

12.DATA DATA MODEL

12.SEC SECURITY MODEL

12.UX STATES

12.AUDIT EVENTS

12.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 13: INTEGRATIONS
================================================================================

The Integrations page is an administrative surface for OAuth apps, MCP
servers, API connectors, webhooks, provider credentials, scopes, health
checks, permissions, and lifecycle. It must operate within the selected
tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never become a
normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/integrations. The
server must require the dedicated permission for Integrations before
returning page data. The permission check must be repeated by every
mutation endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for
UX but is never sufficient for authorization.

13.001 Integrations requirement 1: Test authorization both through
normal navigation and direct API invocation; both paths must produce the
same security decision. For this page, apply that rule specifically to
OAuth apps, MCP servers, API connectors, webhooks, provider credentials,
scopes, health checks, permissions, and lifecycle, using the selected
integrations context where applicable. 13.002 Integrations requirement
2: Make the page resilient to provider outages by retaining
configuration visibility while clearly disabling unavailable runtime
actions. For this page, apply that rule specifically to OAuth apps, MCP
servers, API connectors, webhooks, provider credentials, scopes, health
checks, permissions, and lifecycle, using the selected integrations
context where applicable. 13.003 Integrations requirement 3:

authorization errors, and internal failures. For this page, apply that

using the selected integrations context where applicable. 13.004
Integrations requirement 4: Support safe concurrency by detecting

servers, API connectors, webhooks, provider credentials, scopes, health
checks, permissions, and lifecycle, using the selected integrations
context where applicable. 13.005 Integrations requirement 5: Provide

13.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/integrations must return only fields permitted by the
caller’s administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose
pagination metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request
against the latest resource version and must return an operation
identifier when work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s
standard error envelope and must not expose provider credentials or
protected implementation details.

13.AUDIT EVENTS

13.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 14: CUSTOM CODE
================================================================================

The Custom Code page is an administrative surface for server functions,
scripts, packages, runtimes, builds, environment variables, deployment
policies, logs, and code governance. It must operate within the selected
tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never become a
normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/custom-code. The server
must require the dedicated permission for Custom Code before returning
page data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation
endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but
is never sufficient for authorization.

14.001 Custom Code requirement 1: Support safe concurrency by detecting
competing edits and avoiding silent last-write-wins for critical
settings. For this page, apply that rule specifically to server
functions, scripts, packages, runtimes, builds, environment variables,
deployment policies, logs, and code governance, using the selected
custom code context where applicable. 14.002 Custom Code requirement 2:

users, projects, agents, or deployments. For this page, apply that rule

custom code context where applicable. 14.144 Custom Code requirement
144: Record provider request identifiers when returned so operational

applicable.

14.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/custom-code must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

14.SEC SECURITY MODEL

14.UX STATES

14.AUDIT EVENTS

14.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 15: GITHUB SYNC
================================================================================

The GitHub Sync page is an administrative surface for repositories,
branches, credentials, sync direction, commits, pull requests,
deployments, conflict handling, and synchronization policy. It must
operate within the selected tenant, workspace, environment, and role
scope and must never become a normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/github-sync. The server
must require the dedicated permission for GitHub Sync before returning
page data. The permission check must be repeated by every mutation
endpoint used by the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but
is never sufficient for authorization.

15.001 GitHub Sync requirement 1: Keep administrative page telemetry
free of secret values, authentication headers, raw tokens, and
unnecessary personal data. For this page, apply that rule specifically
to repositories, branches, credentials, sync direction, commits, pull
requests, deployments, conflict handling, and synchronization policy,
using the selected github sync context where applicable. 15.002 GitHub
Sync requirement 2: Include automated tests for normal, unauthorized,

15.API API CONTRACT

GET /admin/github-sync must return only fields permitted by the caller’s
administrative scope. Collection endpoints must expose pagination
metadata. Mutation endpoints must validate the request against the
latest resource version and must return an operation identifier when
work continues asynchronously. Errors must use KYMEX’s standard error
envelope and must not expose provider credentials or protected
implementation details.

15.TEST MATRIX

================================================================================
ADMIN CONSOLE PAGE 16: MONEY
================================================================================

The Money page is an administrative surface for revenue metrics, credit
economics, provider costs, transaction events, refunds, financial
reporting, and operational controls. It must operate within the selected
tenant, workspace, environment, and role scope and must never become a
normal end-user navigation destination.

Use an implementation route equivalent to /admin/money. The server must
require the dedicated permission for Money before returning page data.
The permission check must be repeated by every mutation endpoint used by
the page. A frontend route guard is useful for UX but is never
sufficient for authorization.

16.001 Money requirement 1: Define the page’s primary purpose and
identify which administrative resource is being managed. For this page,
apply that rule specifically to revenue metrics, credit economics,
provider costs, transaction events, refunds, financial reporting, and
operational controls, using the selected money context where applicable.
